# CONF-LIVE-002 — Protected Linux supervisor source candidate

## Status and authority

Alpha 2 is **ONGOING**. This packet is source-only. It neither installs a
launcher nor runs a native campaign. Unit receipts always retain
`nativeAcceptance=false`; a validated receipt is not tenant acceptance.
This document describes the implementation and its acceptance obligations, not
a claim that the obligations have passed. Exact commit/run evidence is recorded
separately at PR closure.

- Packet: CONF-LIVE-002, SHA-256 `05a6f03d19a1e12fafe278e5ec72c8adc6304766697b8682bc8d63092d47427f`.
- Meta authority: `3b1ea6b9b8514fbb51e37ba4703f5d06298bd938`.
- Immediate predecessor: CONF-LIVE-001, main `d734b80a3a5acebfd0e0d289437c0f53f3539054`,
  tree `5cf9cb3e1b286f6c16dcf77ccb4b5524ff568a44`, 120 files / 216 tests.
- Guide SHA-256: `1f1e62240ad6007c962e0a792787a6e5c569f6f72e3fde461868f72c77f034e0`.
- Trusted live-runner contract SHA-256:
  `69690c7bbe0899798f0c39e34e33c13dcd11fca470b8806be2c9c749a8622507`.
- Exactly seven additive paths. No predecessor, dispatcher, workflow, source
  lock, public schema, signature role or PORTING record changes.

## Components and state

| Component | Dependency and responsibility | State / failure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NativeSupervisor | Fixed installed launcher custody, three independent signature roles, exact signed files and kit, native adapter | One session per dedicated process; no caller context or backend argument |
| Native channel | Owned socketpair; per-message kernel credentials; pidfd, PID/start/cgroup/UID/GID/capability/namespace checks | Validate before and after blocking operations; death/EOF/cancellation invalidates handle |
| Cgroup lease | Preallocated empty root-owned cgroup-v2 leaf, finite CPU/memory/PID limits, exclusive session lock | Attach before releasing child gate; kill whole leaf, drain adopted descendants, prove populated=0 |
| Replay journal | Root-only no-follow file, exclusive flock, chained canonical JSONL, append + fsync + readback | RESERVED before child creation, RUNNING before operations, terminal only after cleanup |
| UnitSupervisor / UnitBoundary / UnitReplayStore | Explicit injected unit adapters | UNIT_VERIFICATION_ONLY, never installed authority |
| Fixed proxy successor | CONF-LIVE-003, later protected network/admission implementation | Missing module is PROTECTED_PROXY_UNAVAILABLE; no fallback |

Lifecycle:

`VALIDATED_DATA -> durable RESERVED -> containment + peer checks -> RUNNING -> COMPLETED / FAILED / CANCELLED / EXPIRED`

Terminal states have no outgoing edge. Only all ten fixed, distinct passing
operations can complete. A failed or unavailable operation closes the session;
an unavailable operation's receipt stays NOT_RUN_ENV_UNAVAILABLE. Cancellation
racing an already executing operation may end in FAILED (interrupted I/O) or
CANCELLED; neither permits reuse. Cleanup failure or clock rollback can leave
the durable record RESERVED/RUNNING **consumed**, with no fabricated terminal
receipt. Journal read errors, torn writes, uncertain fsync/readback, full storage
and custody changes all refuse execution. No truncation, repair, nonce expiry,
rotation or automatic lease recovery is implemented.

An append failure before any byte reaches storage may leave an empty journal
after process restart. That is safe only because execution never precedes a
successful durable reservation. Any complete or partial reservation survives
as consumed or as a globally refusing corrupted journal. Root/operator
tampering, disk rollback, hardware lying about fsync and hostile kernels are
outside this source candidate's trust boundary; production backup/restore must
preserve replay history and never restore a reusable old nonce ledger.

## Native boundary and custody

The production constructor accepts no path, executable, callback, module name,
storage adapter, FD, token or verified flag. It requires Linux, root UID/GID,
one thread, no preexisting children and default SIGCHLD disposition; the
module must be loaded from the exact installed launcher archive and its
independently signed root manifest must verify. The unchanged legacy launcher
continues to refuse live execution until the separately owned CONF-LIVE-006
bridge and external installation gates are complete.

The native entry also rejects ambient credentials, proxy/import variables and
open descriptors beyond nonsocket stdio. Only PATH, HOME, USER, LOGNAME, LANG,
LC_ALL, TZ and the fixed HARNESS_LIVE_EXECUTION_ENVELOPE input may be present;
none is an authorization flag. The child clears that remaining environment.
Manifest/public-key/signature/launcher bytes are read once under no-follow
custody, verified with the unchanged signature/key contract, and retained by
the dedicated process. Repeated internal native constructors use that verified
snapshot; the envelope's launcher digest must equal it.

Both platform and tenant signatures and fixed trust-store digests are verified
before opening any envelope-selected reference, including the capacity file.
Only then may that exact digest-bound capacity reference be read and its
independent CAPACITY_OPERATOR signature and complete binding verified. Invalid
or forged references therefore never reach a file open; none grants execution.

Authority references use retained no-follow directory/file descriptors, exact
root ownership, no write bits, single-link regular files, bounded reads and
inode/metadata rechecks. The signed kit is enumerated independently, with each
file read once. It rejects symlinks, hard links, device crossings, unsupported
objects, writable files, path aliases and inventory/digest drift. No warm
checkout or ambient root filesystem is used as a kit. The source candidate's
Fixed bounds are 4 MiB/file, 64 MiB total file data, 4096 files, 8192 total
entries, 4096 entries per directory and depth 32; the release must fit these
bounds or be unavailable.
A root-owned, nonwritable `rootfs/` inside that exact signed kit is required;
there is no host filesystem fallback. Packaging/prerequisite qualification is
not supplied by this packet.

Before any child checkout or credential access, trusted setup:

1. Creates a supervisor-owned SOCK_SEQPACKET socketpair and a closed child gate;
   marks the dedicated parent a child subreaper.
2. Forks a trusted setup child and attaches it to the exclusively leased cgroup
   before releasing the gate. An ambiguous attach still requires cleanup.
3. Clears supplementary groups and creates user, mount, PID and network
   namespaces; the parent writes exact single-ID UID/GID maps to host 65532.
4. Re-arms parent-death SIGKILL after credential changes. PID-namespace init
   retains a parent pidfd to close the death-before-prctl race even when
   getppid() is zero across the namespace boundary.
5. Makes mount propagation private; recursively bind-mounts the signed rootfs
   read-only/nosuid/nodev and chroots into it. Closes all descriptors except the
   owned local channel; removes capabilities and enables no_new_privs.
6. Installs architecture-checked classic-BPF seccomp rules. Direct socket
   creation/connect/bind/listen, namespace escape, ptrace, BPF, userfaultfd,
   io_uring and pidfd_getfd paths are denied on amd64/arm64. x32 is rejected.
   clone3 returns ENOSYS; ordinary clone/fork is allowed only inside the
   PID namespace, limits and cgroup. This is a layered boundary, not a claim
   that a syscall denylist alone is a complete sandbox.
7. Reports READY through per-message SCM_CREDENTIALS. The parent checks the
   child's kernel identity, exact cgroup, distinct namespaces, mapped UID/GID,
   zero capability sets, seccomp and no_new_privs, and retains a pidfd.

SO_PEERCRED from socketpair creation is deliberately not used as post-fork
sender identity. Truncated, duplicate or unexpected ancillary messages fail;
all received SCM_RIGHTS descriptors are closed and never become authority.
Only the supervisor-created channel is used, with checks around every bounded
operation. The isolated custodian presently accepts only a fixed ping byte;
it does **not** yet execute probes or load tenant credentials.

The child uses an absolute monotonic deadline (at most 900 seconds and never
past signed expiry), recalculated before each receive so pings cannot extend
its life. Parent death kills the trusted intermediate parent and namespace
init; PID namespace teardown kills remaining namespace processes. An alive
supervisor's expiry/cancel/close path writes cgroup.kill and drains direct and
adopted children until both wait custody and cgroup emptiness are proved.
Kernel/init reaping after supervisor death is a **native qualification gate**,
not something offline mocks establish. After a parent crash, an occupied
preallocated leaf blocks the next session instead of being silently cleared.

## Operator prerequisites — not performed here

The separately authorized native operator must provide a supported native
64-bit Linux host, Python 3.12.14 and the exact signed launcher, cgroup v2
including cgroup.kill, mount_setattr recursive read-only support, user/mount/
PID/network namespaces, pidfd, prctl/capset/seccomp, and enabled finite CPU/
memory/PID controllers. Unsupported primitives have no subprocess/emulation/
uncontained fallback. Missing prerequisites are NOT_RUN_ENV_UNAVAILABLE, never
PASS; the later bridge owns mapping internal prerequisite exceptions into the
unchanged campaign result format.

Fixed, precreated paths (not user request parameters):

- `/sys/fs/cgroup/planeon-live/session`: empty non-delegated cgroup-v2 domain.
- `/var/lib/planeon/live-backend`: root:root 0700.
- `session.lock` and `replay.jsonl` under that directory: root:root 0600,
  precreated regular files, no hard links. The journal starts empty once;
  never recreate it to clear consumed nonces.
- Existing root manifest, release trust and tenant trust fixed mounts under
  the unchanged trusted live-runner contract.

No install command, sudo policy, cluster/VM provisioning, rootfs creation,
credential import, cloud API, hosted runner or network policy mutation is part
of this source run.

## Handoff to CONF-LIVE-003 and remaining roadmap

The only production transport hook is the fixed relative import
`live_proxy_client.execute_protected(request, installed_context, deadline)`.
It is intentionally absent until its owning packet. Caller data never chooses
a transport/module/URL/argv. The context is constructed only by NativeSupervisor
and retains its owning supervisor, validated binding, plan, envelope, capacity,
source digest and owned rootfs FD. These are private in-process custody data,
not serializable session authority.

The privileged broker is distinct from the isolated child. CONF-LIVE-003 must
independently enforce the single signed endpoint, TLS identity, no discovery/
redirect/proxy environment, bounded request/response/deadline, short-lived
credential custody and server-side zero-cost quota admission. A live peer or
successful ping grants none of those permissions by itself. CONF-LIVE-004
owns the ten real fixed probes. CONF-LIVE-005 owns deterministic candidate
packaging; CONF-LIVE-006 owns the closed integration delta. External operator
installation, native amd64/arm64 Linux, OCP/Kubernetes runtime, assurance and
tenant acceptance remain separate WAITING gates.

## Offline verification and evidence boundaries

Run only the packet's exact eight direct argv through the signed, root-owned
offline launcher in one deny-all-outbound process tree: all six unittest roots,
then campaign linux-baseline and evidence-verify linux-baseline. No separate
test execution or native syscall execution is permitted during coding/CI.

New tests cover reserve-before-child ordering; nonce contention; crashes before,
during and after append/fsync/readback; consumed restart history; replay chain,
scope and timing; peer identity/FD spoofing; per-message credential rejection;
BPF instruction behavior for both ABIs and IPv4/IPv6/DNS/metadata denial;
namespace-clone escape filtering; adapter failure ordering; cgroup attach
ambiguity, adopted-child reaping and incomplete cleanup; one-operation locks,
cancellation, expired/late I/O and rollback; factory injection and unsigned
evidence. Syscalls are explicitly mocked; no test result is native
containment evidence. Inventory tests retain original 103/120, corrected
106/150, successor 110/170 and immediate 120/216 file/test checkpoints, including
fresh isolated discovery of all six roots without omission/skip/xfail.

Keep local-source checks, GitHub CI, PR merge, local exact-main replay,
candidate artifact/SBOM, installed preflight, native runtime, assurance and
tenant acceptance as independent states. Roll back this unconsumed increment
by a reviewed revert; after successor consumption use a corrective packet.
Do not alter installations, tenant resources, trust or replay history.

## Design references

- [Linux cgroup-v2 documentation](https://docs.kernel.org/admin-guide/cgroup-v2.html):
  process inheritance, resource limits and whole-cgroup kill behavior.
- [Linux seccomp filter documentation](https://www.kernel.org/doc/html/latest/userspace-api/seccomp_filter.html):
  architecture checks, no_new_privs and layered confinement.
- [Unix-domain socket credentials](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/unix.7.html):
  per-message SCM_CREDENTIALS versus socket-creation credentials.
- [User namespaces](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/user_namespaces.7.html):
  UID/GID mapping and supplementary-group ordering.
- [Parent-death signal](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/PR_SET_PDEATHSIG.2const.html)
  and [PID namespaces](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/pid_namespaces.7.html):
  re-arming, death races and PID-namespace init teardown.
- [Recursive mount attributes](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/mount_setattr.2.html):
  recursive read-only/nosuid/nodev enforcement.

## CONF-FIX-004 — retained custody correction

Alpha 2 remains ONGOING. This is a SOURCE-ONLY candidate. Local acceptance,
required localhost CI, merge and independent local exact-main are separate
pending gates until their exact commit/run records are published. No native
execution, installation, server containment or tenant acceptance is asserted.

Authority: MET-REPAIR-011 at meta main
7447acd1a0471cecb79da36abe6b4ff0859f0a92, canonical amendment SHA-256
26d0301045c60908850ec225fa497d73da4c4125c377e74a931d41c83d57c491.
Original conformance checkpoint: 7205075d2f234b622dd61072803b754f1dffeb79,
tree c48d71a7c8d5ddf245e1aab8063ecd9ae2b59834, 127 files / 279 tests.
The fence below retains that checkpoint as history, not corrected file hashes.

### Ownership and handoff

The fixed NativeSupervisor constructor creates one process/thread-bound internal
registry. A cached manifest digest, mocked reader return, caller FD, subclass,
foreign owner or deserialized object cannot complete its authority inventory.
Standalone legacy byte readers remain data-only and retain their signatures.

The registry retains every no-follow directory in the full chain, each regular
file FD and immutable bytes, and the named inode plus type/owner/mode/link/size/
mtime/ctime snapshot. Installed identity is verified once into that registry;
subsequent checks do not reopen any path or silently refresh trust. The signed
kit is enumerated once. Its verified rootfs directory is borrowed, not reopened.

InstalledContext carries read-only _bytes (absolute retained authority paths)
and _kit (signed kit-relative bytes), plus original signed authority and a
binding/plan snapshot. The fixed proxy successor consumes release/profile/CA/
observation-binding bytes from these maps only. These internal objects are not
public request APIs, independent signer proof, or native acceptance evidence.
The proxy must check the genuine active owner/channel before any credential
or network operation and keep its own server-side admission requirements.

A durable nonce reservation precedes containment. Every peer/request/hook
boundary rechecks retained custody, all three signer roles/windows and signed
scope. Wall and monotonic time cannot roll back or extend the original deadline;
checks repeat after blocking channel and proxy operations. Changed authority
fails closed and is not re-enrolled. The fixed fork setup remains unchanged:
only its required rootfs/channel are used; all other descriptors are closed
before isolated custodian operation. No authority data is sent over that channel.

### Bounds and failure behavior

Existing kit limits remain 4 MiB/file, 64 MiB kit data, 4096 kit files,
8192 kit entries, 4096 directory entries and kit depth 32. The complete registry
adds finite ceilings of 4112 files, 8448 descriptors, 96 MiB retained file bytes,
64 absolute path components and 4096 UTF-8 path bytes. Descriptors must be above
stdio and below the unchanged child's close-range ceiling. Kernel descriptor
exhaustion refuses immediately; no RLIMIT increase, reopen or partial fallback.

Registry invalidation precedes close attempts. Each descriptor number is removed
from ownership before a close can report failure and is never retried. A replaced
descriptor is rejected without closing the unrelated replacement. Cleanup
continues after individual errors. Clock failures cannot prevent containment
cleanup. An ambiguous reservation consumes the instance; consumed journal
history is never reset. Unproven cleanup/time leaves no successful terminal
record. Parent/native crash recovery remains a separately qualified Linux gate.

### Regression and evidence boundaries

Append-only regressions exercise the actual native factory, signature and
custody checks, reservation, channel and fixed-hook dispatch using explicit
in-memory OS observations. They do not patch an eligibility check to PASS.
They cover all retained paths and ancestor metadata; read-once/rootfs reuse;
each signer's expiry/revocation; forged/foreign/forked contexts; exhaustion and
read errors; post-I/O substitution; cancellation, clock failure, ambiguous
reservation and close-error cleanup. All results remain UNIT_VERIFICATION_ONLY.

The appended stdlib-only source guard reuses the pinned data-only region oracle,
checks all five corrected files and every unchanged file in the original
127-path inventory, preserves all original test/document bytes, and compares
fresh unittest collection to AST identity lists in all six roots. Stored source
text is never compiled, imported or executed.

(0xC000003E, 56, (41, 42, 49, 50, 101, 165, 166, 272, 308, 321, 323, 425, 426, 427, 435, 438)),\\n          \\\"aarch64\\\": (0xC00000B7, 220, (198, 203, 200, 201, 117, 40, 39, 97, 268, 280, 282, 425, 426, 427, 435, 438))}\\nNAMESPACE_MASK = CLONE_NEWUSER | CLONE_NEWNS | CLONE_NEWPID | CLONE_NEWNET | 0x04000000 | 0x08000000 | 0x02000000\\n_VERIFIED_MANIFEST = None\\n\\n\\ndef require(condition, reason):\\n    if not condition:\\n        raise ConformanceError(reason, \\\"protected Linux boundary refused\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef installed_process():\\n    # No flag, argv spoof or UID alone establishes installed code custody.\\n    require(sys.platform == \\\"linux\\\", \\\"LINUX_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n    require(os.geteuid() == 0 and os.getegid() == 0, \\\"LINUX_ROOT_REQUIRED\\\")\\n    require(not any(os.environ.get(k) for k in (\\\"CI\\\", \\\"GITHUB_ACTIONS\\\", \\\"GITHUB_EVENT_NAME\\\", \\\"BUILDKITE\\\", \\\"JENKINS_URL\\\")),\\n            \\\"CI_EXECUTION_FORBIDDEN\\\")\\n    from .live import EXPECTED_LAUNCHER\\n    loader = globals().get(\\\"__loader__\\\")\\n    require(type(getattr(loader, \\\"archive\\\", None)) is str and loader.archive == str(EXPECTED_LAUNCHER)\\n            and sys.argv[0] == str(EXPECTED_LAUNCHER), \\\"INSTALLED_CODE_CUSTODY_REQUIRED\\\")\\n    require(len(os.listdir(\\\"/proc/self/task\\\")) == 1, \\\"SINGLE_THREAD_SUPERVISOR_REQUIRED\\\")\\n    require(not Path(f\\\"/proc/self/task/{os.getpid()}/children\\\").read_text().strip()\\n            and signal.getsignal(signal.SIGCHLD) == signal.SIG_DFL, \\\"DEDICATED_SUPERVISOR_REQUIRED\\\")\\n    return _installed_manifest_digest()\\n\\n\\ndef _installed_manifest_digest():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Retain independently verified installed bytes; never re-open to execute.\\n\\n    Same closed manifest/key contract as the immutable predecessor launcher.\\n    Internal per-process custody is not a caller-supplied verified flag.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    global _VERIFIED_MANIFEST\\n    if _VERIFIED_MANIFEST is not None and _VERIFIED_MANIFEST[0] == os.getpid():\\n        return _VERIFIED_MANIFEST[1]\\n    from .live import (EXPECTED_LAUNCHER, FIXED_MANIFEST, FIXED_MANIFEST_PUBLIC,\\n                      FIXED_MANIFEST_SIGNATURE, PINNED_ROOT_PUBLIC_KEY_SHA256)\\n    public_raw = read_owned(str(FIXED_MANIFEST_PUBLIC), expected_digest=PINNED_ROOT_PUBLIC_KEY_SHA256)\\n    public_record = load_json_bytes(public_raw)\\n    closed(public_record, (\\\"algorithm\\\", \\\"publicKey\\\"))\\n    require(public_record[\\\"algorithm\\\"] == \\\"ED25519\\\", \\\"ROOT_KEY_ALGORITHM\\\")\\n    raw = read_owned(str(FIXED_MANIFEST))\\n    signature = b64url_decode(read_owned(str(FIXED_MANIFEST_SIGNATURE)).decode(\\\"ascii\\\").strip(), expected_length=64)\\n    public = b64url_decode(public_record[\\\"publicKey\\\"], expected_length=32)\\n    require(verify(public, raw, signature), \\\"ROOT_MANIFEST_SIGNATURE_INVALID\\\")\\n    manifest = load_json_bytes(raw)\\n    closed(manifest, (\\\"schemaVersion\\\", \\\"launcher\\\", \\\"fixedTrustMounts\\\", \\\"isolation\\\", \\\"preflightEvidenceDigest\\\"))\\n    require(manifest[\\\"schemaVersion\\\"] == \\\"harness.planeon.ai/live-runner-manifest/v1alpha1\\\", \\\"ROOT_MANIFEST_SCHEMA\\\")\\n    launcher_raw = read_owned(str(EXPECTED_LAUNCHER), expected_mode=0o555)\\n    digest = byte_digest(launcher_raw)\\n    require(manifest[\\\"launcher\\\"] == {\\\"path\\\": str(EXPECTED_LAUNCHER), \\\"version\\\": \\\"0.1.0\\\", \\\"sha256\\\": digest,\\n            \\\"ownerUid\\\": 0, \\\"ownerGid\\\": 0, \\\"mode\\\": \\\"0555\\\"}, \\\"LAUNCHER_CUSTODY_INVALID\\\")\\n    # read_owned checked actual owner/mode custody. Retain the source digest;\\n    # no later pathname becomes authority.\\n    require(manifest[\\\"fixedTrustMounts\\\"] == [\\\"/etc/planeon/trust/release-trust-bundle.json\\\",\\n            \\\"/etc/planeon/trust/tenant-trust-bundle.json\\\"], \\\"TRUST_MOUNTS_INVALID\\\")\\n    require(manifest[\\\"isolation\\\"] == {\\\"backend\\\": \\\"PREINSTALLED_OS_ENDPOINT_ALLOWLIST_V1\\\",\\n            \\\"networkPolicy\\\": \\\"DENY_ALL_EXCEPT_DUAL_SIGNED_ENDPOINTS\\\", \\\"credentialSocketsDenied\\\": True,\\n            \\\"ciDenied\\\": True}, \\\"ISOLATION_MANIFEST_INVALID\\\")\\n    _VERIFIED_MANIFEST = (os.getpid(), digest)\\n    return digest\\n\\n\\ndef ambient_custody():\\n    # Only fixed launcher input plus non-authorizing locale/stdio metadata.\\n    # Short-lived credentials are opened later from signed references, never env.\\n    allowed = {\\\"PATH\\\", \\\"HOME\\\", \\\"USER\\\", \\\"LOGNAME\\\", \\\"LANG\\\", \\\"LC_ALL\\\", \\\"TZ\\\", \\\"HARNESS_LIVE_EXECUTION_ENVELOPE\\\"}\\n    require(not set(os.environ) - allowed, \\\"AMBIENT_ENVIRONMENT_FORBIDDEN\\\")\\n    for name in os.listdir(\\\"/proc/self/fd\\\"):\\n        require(name.isdigit(), \\\"AMBIENT_DESCRIPTOR_FORBIDDEN\\\")\\n        fd = int(name)\\n        try:\\n            info = os.fstat(fd)\\n        except OSError as exc:\\n            # listdir's transient descriptor has already been closed.\\n            if exc.errno == errno.EBADF:\\n                continue\\n            raise\\n        require(fd <= 2 and not stat.S_ISSOCK(info.st_mode), \\\"AMBIENT_DESCRIPTOR_FORBIDDEN\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef read_owned(path, maximum=4194304, expected_digest=None, expected_mode=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Retained no-follow ancestry and exact inode/metadata/read-only custody.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    require(type(path) is str and path.startswith(\\\"/\\\") and \\\"\\\\\\\\\\\" not in path\\n            and all(p not in (\\\"\\\", \\\".\\\", \\\"..\\\") for p in path[1:].split(\\\"/\\\")),\\n            \\\"CUSTODY_PATH_INVALID\\\")\\n    parent = open_directory(str(Path(path).parent))\\n    fd = None\\n    try:\\n        fd = os.open(Path(path).name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=parent)\\n        before = os.fstat(fd)\\n        require(stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) and before.st_uid == before.st_gid == 0\\n                and before.st_nlink == 1 and not stat.S_IMODE(before.st_mode) & 0o222\\n                and before.st_size <= maximum, \\\"CUSTODY_FILE_INVALID\\\")\\n        require(expected_mode is None or stat.S_IMODE(before.st_mode) == expected_mode, \\\"CUSTODY_MODE_INVALID\\\")\\n        raw = bytearray()\\n        while len(raw) <= maximum:\\n            chunk = os.read(fd, min(65536, maximum + 1 - len(raw)))\\n            if not chunk:\\n                break\\n            raw.extend(chunk)\\n        named, after = os.stat(Path(path).name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False), os.fstat(fd)\\n        identity = lambda s: (s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_uid, s.st_gid, s.st_mode, s.st_nlink,\\n                              s.st_size, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_ctime_ns)\\n        require(len(raw) == before.st_size and identity(before) == identity(after) == identity(named),\\n                \\\"CUSTODY_FILE_CHANGED\\\")\\n        raw = bytes(raw)\\n        require(expected_digest is None or byte_digest(raw) == expected_digest, \\\"CUSTODY_DIGEST_MISMATCH\\\")\\n        return raw\\n    finally:\\n        if fd is not None:\\n            os.close(fd)\\n        os.close(parent)\\n\\n\\ndef read_owned_kit(root, expected_digest):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Read each signed kit file once, from retained no-follow directory FDs.\\n\\n    Reject mount crossings, hard links, writable/extraneous files and unbounded\\n    inventories. Returning bytes is verification data, not an execution grant.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    root_fd = open_directory(root)\\n    rows, sources = [], {}\\n    total, entries = 0, 0\\n    device = os.fstat(root_fd).st_dev\\n    def walk(directory, prefix, depth):\\n        nonlocal total, entries\\n        require(depth <= 32, \\\"KIT_DEPTH_EXCEEDED\\\")\\n        before = os.fstat(directory)\\n        require(before.st_dev == device and before.st_uid == before.st_gid == 0\\n                and not stat.S_IMODE(before.st_mode) & 0o222, \\\"KIT_MOUNT_OR_CUSTODY_INVALID\\\")\\n        names = sorted(os.listdir(directory), key=lambda name: name.encode(\\\"utf-8\\\"))\\n        require(len(names) <= 4096, \\\"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\\\")\\n        entries += len(names)\\n        require(entries <= 8192, \\\"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\\\")\\n        for name in names:\\n            require(name not in (\\\"\\\", \\\".\\\", \\\"..\\\") and \\\"/\\\" not in name and \\\"\\\\\\\\\\\" not in name, \\\"KIT_PATH_INVALID\\\")\\n            info = os.stat(name, dir_fd=directory, follow_symlinks=False)\\n            require(info.st_uid == info.st_gid == 0 and info.st_dev == device\\n                    and not stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) & 0o222, \\\"KIT_CUSTODY_INVALID\\\")\\n            relative = prefix + name\\n            if stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):\\n                fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=directory)\\n                try:\\n                    require((os.fstat(fd).st_dev, os.fstat(fd).st_ino) == (info.st_dev, info.st_ino), \\\"KIT_CHANGED\\\")\\n                    walk(fd, relative + \\\"/\\\", depth + 1)\\n                finally:\\n                    os.close(fd)\\n            else:\\n                require(stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) and info.st_nlink == 1 and 0 <= info.st_size <= 4194304,\\n                        \\\"KIT_FILE_INVALID\\\")\\n                require(len(rows) < 4096 and total + info.st_size <= 67108864, \\\"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\\\")\\n                fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=directory)\\n                try:\\n                    identity = lambda s: (s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_uid, s.st_gid, s.st_mode,\\n                                          s.st_nlink, s.st_size, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_ctime_ns)\\n                    require(identity(os.fstat(fd)) == identity(info), \\\"KIT_CHANGED\\\")\\n                    raw = bytearray()\\n                    while len(raw) <= info.st_size:\\n                        chunk = os.read(fd, min(65536, info.st_size + 1 - len(raw)))\\n                        if not chunk:\\n                            break\\n                        raw.extend(chunk)\\n                    require(len(raw) == info.st_size and identity(os.fstat(fd)) == identity(info)\\n                            == identity(os.stat(name, dir_fd=directory, follow_symlinks=False)), \\\"KIT_CHANGED\\\")\\n                finally:\\n                    os.close(fd)\\n                sources[relative] = bytes(raw)\\n                total += len(raw)\\n                rows.append(dict(mode=f\\\"{stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode):04o}\\\", path=relative,\\n                                 sha256=byte_digest(bytes(raw)), size=len(raw)))\\n        after = os.fstat(directory)\\n        require((before.st_dev, before.st_ino, before.st_mtime_ns, before.st_ctime_ns) ==\\n                (after.st_dev, after.st_ino, after.st_mtime_ns, after.st_ctime_ns), \\\"KIT_CHANGED\\\")\\n    try:\\n        walk(root_fd, \\\"\\\", 0)\\n        rows.sort(key=lambda row: row[\\\"path\\\"].encode(\\\"utf-8\\\"))\\n        require(rows and canonical_digest(rows, \\\"planeon.harness-live-tree/v1alpha1\\\") == expected_digest,\\n                \\\"KIT_DIGEST_MISMATCH\\\")\\n        return sources\\n    finally:\\n        os.close(root_fd)\\n\\n\\ndef seccomp_program(machine):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Classic BPF with architecture check, x32 rejection and namespace denial.\\n\\n    Direct network creation/connect/bind/listen is denied. Only the inherited\\n    supervisor-owned local channel survives; no raw CRI/network socket grant.\\n    Ordinary fork/exec remains possible inside the cgroup and PID namespace.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    require(machine in ARCHES, \\\"LINUX_ARCHITECTURE_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n    arch, clone, denied = ARCHES[machine]\\n    load, equal, bits, ret = 0x20, 0x15, 0x45, 0x06\\n    kill, allow, refuse = 0x80000000, 0x7FFF0000, 0x00050000 | errno.EPERM\\n    code = [(load, 0, 0, 4), (equal, 1, 0, arch), (ret, 0, 0, kill), (load, 0, 0, 0)]\\n    if machine == \\\"x86_64\\\":\\n        code += [(bits, 0, 1, 0x40000000), (ret, 0, 0, kill)]\\n    for number in denied:\\n        # clone3 pointer flags cannot be inspected by classic BPF; ENOSYS\\n        # permits libc's ordinary clone fallback, still filtered below.\\n        action = (0x00050000 | errno.ENOSYS) if number == 435 else refuse\\n        code += [(equal, 0, 1, number), (ret, 0, 0, action)]\\n    code += [(equal, 0, 3, clone), (load, 0, 0, 16), (bits, 0, 1, NAMESPACE_MASK),\\n             (ret, 0, 0, refuse), (ret, 0, 0, allow)]\\n    return tuple(code)\\n\\n\\nclass SockFilter(ctypes.Structure):\\n    _fields_ = [(\\\"code\\\", ctypes.c_ushort), (\\\"jt\\\", ctypes.c_ubyte), (\\\"jf\\\", ctypes.c_ubyte), (\\\"k\\\", ctypes.c_uint)]\\n\\n\\nclass SockFprog(ctypes.Structure):\\n    _fields_ = [(\\\"len\\\", ctypes.c_ushort), (\\\"filter\\\", ctypes.POINTER(SockFilter))]\\n\\n\\nclass CapHeader(ctypes.Structure):\\n    _fields_ = [(\\\"version\\\", ctypes.c_uint), (\\\"pid\\\", ctypes.c_int)]\\n\\n\\nclass CapData(ctypes.Structure):\\n    _fields_ = [(\\\"effective\\\", ctypes.c_uint), (\\\"permitted\\\", ctypes.c_uint), (\\\"inheritable\\\", ctypes.c_uint)]\\n\\n\\nclass MountAttr(ctypes.Structure):\\n ctypes.c_ulonglong)]\\n\\n\\nclass StatFS(ctypes.Structure):\\n    _fields_ = [(\\\"type\\\", ctypes.c_long), (\\\"bsize\\\", ctypes.c_long),\\n                (\\\"blocks\\\", ctypes.c_ulonglong), 2),\\n                (\\\"namelen\\\", ctypes.c_long), (\\\"frsize\\\", ctypes.c_long), (\\\"flags\\\", ctypes.c_long),\\n                (\\\"spare\\\", ctypes.c_long * 4)]\\n\\n\\nclass LinuxSyscalls:\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        installed_process()\\n        self.machine = os.uname().machine\\n        require(self.machine in ARCHES, \\\"LINUX_ARCHITECTURE_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n        self.libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)\\n        declarations = {\\\"unshare\\\": ([ctypes.c_int], ctypes.c_int),\\n                        \\\"prctl\\\": ([ctypes.c_int] + [ctypes.c_ulong] * 4, ctypes.c_int),\\n                        \\\"mount\\\": ([ctypes.c_char_p] * 3 + [ctypes.c_ulong, ctypes.c_void_p], ctypes.c_int),\\n                        \\\"capset\\\": ([ctypes.POINTER(CapHeader), ctypes.POINTER(CapData)], ctypes.c_int),\\n                        \\\"fstatfs\\\": ([ctypes.c_int, ctypes.POINTER(StatFS)], ctypes.c_int)}\\n        for name, (args, result) in declarations.items():\\n            function = getattr(self.libc, name)\\n            function.argtypes, function.restype = args, result\\n        # Fixed-number mount_setattr(442) on both supported 64-bit Linux ABIs.\\n        self.libc.syscall.restype = ctypes.c_long\\n\\n    def call(self, name, *args):\\n        result = getattr(self.libc, name)(*args)\\n        if result < 0:\\n            raise OSError(ctypes.get_errno(), \\\"required Linux primitive unavailable\\\")\\n        return result\\n\\n    def prctl(self, option, value):\\n        self.call(\\\"prctl\\\", option, value, 0, 0, 0)\\n\\n    def protect_parent(self, parent_pid):\\n        self.prctl(PR_SET_PDEATHSIG, signal.SIGKILL)\\n        require(os.getppid() == parent_pid, \\\"SUPERVISOR_PARENT_DIED\\\")\\n\\n    def namespaces(self):\\n        self.call(\\\"unshare\\\", CLONE_NEWUSER | CLONE_NEWNS | CLONE_NEWPID | CLONE_NEWNET)\\n\\n    def require_cgroup2(self, fd):\\n        info = StatFS()\\n        self.call(\\\"fstatfs\\\", fd, ctypes.byref(info))\\n        require(info.type == 0x63677270, \\\"CGROUP_FILESYSTEM_REQUIRED\\\")\\n\\n    def readonly_root(self, root_fd):\\n        self.call(\\\"mount\\\", None, b\\\"/\\\", None, MS_REC | MS_PRIVATE, None)\\n        os.fchdir(root_fd)\\n        self.call(\\\"mount\\\", b\\\".\\\", b\\\".\\\", None, MS_BIND | MS_REC, None)\\n        attrs = MountAttr(1 | 2 | 4, 0, 0, 0)  # RDONLY, NOSUID, NODEV recursively\\n        self.call(\\\"syscall\\\", ctypes.c_long(442), os.chdir(\\\"/\\\")\\n\\n    def drop_privileges(self):\\n        self.prctl(PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS, 1)\\n        self.prctl(PR_SET_DUMPABLE, 0)\\n        for capability in range(64):\\n            try:\\n                self.prctl(PR_CAPBSET_DROP, capability)\\n            except OSError as exc:\\n                if exc.errno != errno.EINVAL:\\n                    raise\\n        header, data = CapHeader(0x20080522, 0), (CapData * 2)()\\n        self.call(\\\"capset\\\", ctypes.byref(header), data)\\n\\n    def seccomp(self):\\n        rules = seccomp_program(self.machine)\\n        filters 0)\\n\\n\\ndef process_identity(pid):\\n    require(type(pid) is int and pid > 1, \\\"PEER_PID_INVALID\\\")\\n    # proc magic links below are fixed kernel identities, never caller paths.\\n    prefix = Path(\\\"/proc\\\") / str(pid)\\n    raw = (prefix / \\\"stat\\\").read_text()\\n    end = raw.rfind(\\\")\\\")\\n    require(end > 0 and raw[:raw.find(\\\" \\\")] == str(pid), \\\"PEER_STAT_INVALID\\\")\\n    fields = raw[end + 2:].split()\\n    require(len(fields) >= 20, \\\"PEER_STAT_INVALID\\\")\\n    status = dict(line.split(\\\":\\\", 1) for line in (prefix / \\\"status\\\").read_text().splitlines() if \\\":\\\" in line)\\n    cgroup = (prefix / \\\"cgroup\\\").read_text()\\n    return {\\\"pid\\\": pid, \\\"start\\\": int(fields[19]), \\\"parent\\\": int(fields[1]),\\n            \\\"uid\\\": tuple(map(int, status[\\\"Uid\\\"].split())), \\\"gid\\\": tuple(map(int, status[\\\"Gid\\\"].split())),\\n            \\\"capabilities\\\": \\\"noNewPrivs\\\": int(status[\\\"NoNewPrivs\\\"]),\\n            \\\"cgroup\\\": cgroup, \\\"namespaces\\\": {name: (prefix / \\\"ns\\\" / name).stat().st_ino\\n                for name in (\\\"user\\\", \\\"mnt\\\", \\\"pid\\\", \\\"net\\\")}}\\n\\n\\ndef validate_peer(observed, expected, parent_namespaces):\\n    require(observed == expected and observed[\\\"pid\\\"] > 1 and observed[\\\"start\\\"] > 0,\\n            \\\"PEER_IDENTITY_CHANGED\\\")\\n    require(observed[\\\"uid\\\"] == (CHILD_UID,) * 4 and observed[\\\"gid\\\"] == (CHILD_GID,) * 4\\n            and observed[\\\"capabilities\\\"] == (0,) * 5 and observed[\\\"seccomp\\\"] == 2\\n            and observed[\\\"noNewPrivs\\\"] == 1, \\\"PEER_PRIVILEGE_INVALID\\\")\\n    require(observed[\\\"cgroup\\\"] == \\\"0::/planeon-live/session\\\\n\\\", \\\"PEER_CGROUP_MISMATCH\\\")\\n    require(set(observed[\\\"namespaces\\\"]) == {\\\"user\\\", \\\"mnt\\\", \\\"pid\\\", \\\"net\\\"}\\n            and all(observed[\\\"namespaces\\\"][k] != parent_namespaces[k] for k in parent_namespaces),\\n            \\\"PEER_NAMESPACE_MISMATCH\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef credentials(ancillary, flags):\\n    # Close every received descriptor even when another malformed message is\\n    # encountered first. Ancillary data never constitutes a capability grant.\\n    values, unexpected = [], False\\n    for level, kind, raw in ancillary:\\n        if level == socket.SOL_SOCKET and kind == socket.SCM_RIGHTS:\\n            fds = array.array(\\\"i\\\")\\n            fds.frombytes(raw[:len(raw) - len(raw) % fds.itemsize])\\n            for fd in fds:\\n                try:\\n                    os.close(fd)\\n                except OSError:\\n                    unexpected = True\\n            unexpected = True\\n        elif level == socket.SOL_SOCKET and kind == 2 and len(raw) == 12: not unexpected and len(values) == 1,\\n            \\\"PEER_CHANNEL_INVALID\\\")\\n    return values[0]\\n\\n\\nclass CgroupLease:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Use one preallocated empty leaf under an exclusive operator-owned lease.\\n\\n    No cgroup or resource provisioning; never kill by recycled numeric PID.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        native = LinuxSyscalls()\\n        self.fd = open_directory(CGROUP)\\n        self.closed = False\\n        self.owned = False\\n        try:\\n            native.require_cgroup2(self.fd)\\n            require(self.read(\\\"cgroup.type\\\") == b\\\"domain\\\\n\\\", \\\"CGROUP_TYPE_INVALID\\\")\\n            require(self.read(\\\"cgroup.events\\\").splitlines().count(b\\\"populated 0\\\") == 1, \\\"CGROUP_NOT_EMPTY\\\")\\n            for name in (\\\"memory.max\\\", \\\"pids.max\\\"):\\n                raw = self.read(name).strip()\\n                require(raw.isdigit() and int(raw) > 0, \\\"CGROUP_LIMIT_REQUIRED\\\")\\n            cpu = self.read(\\\"cpu.max\\\").split()\\n            require(len(cpu) == 2 and all(v.isdigit() and int(v) > 0 for v in cpu), \\\"CGROUP_LIMIT_REQUIRED\\\")\\n        except BaseException:\\n \\\"CGROUP_FIELD_INVALID\\\")\\n        fd = os.open(name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=self.fd)\\n        try:\\n            self._field_custody(fd)\\n            return os.read(fd, 4096)\\n        finally:\\n            os.close(fd)\\n\\n    def write(self, name, value):\\n        require(name in (\\\"cgroup.procs\\\", \\\"cgroup.kill\\\") and type(value) is bytes, require(os.write(fd, value) == len(value), \\\"CGROUP_WRITE_FAILED\\\")\\n        finally:\\n            os.close(fd)\\n\\n    def _field_custody(self, fd):\\n        info = os.fstat(fd)\\n        require(stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) and info.st_uid == info.st_gid == 0\\n                and info.st_dev == os.fstat(self.fd).st_dev and not stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) & 0o022,\\n                \\\"CGROUP_FIELD_CUSTODY_INVALID\\\")\\n\\n    def attach(self, pid):\\n        require(type(pid) is int and pid > 1 and not self.owned, \\\"CGROUP_ATTACH_INVALID\\\")\\n        # Custody is held even when the kernel write's result is ambiguous.\\n        # The child gate is still closed; cleanup kills only this leased leaf.\\n        self.owned = True\\n        self.write(\\\"cgroup.procs\\\", str(pid).encode())\\n\\n    def kill_and_reap(self, child_pid, deadline):\\n        require(self.owned and not self.closed, \\\"CGROUP_NOT_OWNED\\\")\\n        self.write(\\\"cgroup.kill\\\", b\\\"1\\\")\\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\\n            # This dedicated, single-thread subreaper had no children before\\n            # its gated fork. Drain direct AND adopted descendants, not just\\n            # the first child. PID values here are wait identities, not kill.\\n            no_children = False\\n            while True:\\n                try:\\n                    pid, _ = os.waitpid(-1, os.WNOHANG)\\n                    if pid == 0:\\n                        break\\n                except ChildProcessError:\\n                    no_children = True\\n                    break\\n            if b\\\"populated 0\\\" in self.read(\\\"cgroup.events\\\").splitlines() and no_children:\\n                self.close()\\n                return\\n            time.sleep(0.01)\\n        raise ConformanceError(\\\"DESCENDANTS_NOT_REAPED\\\", \\\"cleanup did not prove an empty process tree\\\")\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        if not self.closed:\\n            os.close(self.fd)\\n            self.closed = True\\n\\n\\nclass UnitBoundary:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Deterministic dependency-injected ordering model, never native authority.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    evidence_class = \\\"UNIT_VERIFICATION_ONLY\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, syscalls):\\n        self.syscalls = syscalls\\n\\n    def establish(self):\\n        for step in (\\\"parent_death\\\", \\\"cgroup_attach\\\", \\\"user_mount_pid_network_namespaces\\\", \\\"uid_gid_maps\\\",\\n                     \\\"readonly_root\\\", \\\"close_ambient_fds\\\", \\\"drop_capabilities\\\", \\\"no_new_privs\\\", \\\"seccomp\\\", \\\"peer_check\\\"):\\n            self.syscalls.step(step)\\n        return {\\\"evidenceClass\\\": self.evidence_class, \\\"nativeAcceptance\\\": False}\\n\",\n    \"src/harness_conformance/live_supervisor.py\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Protected supervisor source candidate, not an installed/native qualification.\\n\\nThe native constructor is fixed and custody-checked. UnitSupervisor is a\\nseparate non-authorizing model; its handles cannot enter NativeSupervisor.\\nNo source-selected backend, executable, shell, credential or socket path API.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\\nimport fcntl\\nimport os\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nimport select\\nimport signal\\nimport socket\\nimport stat\\nimport threading\\nimport time\\n\\nfrom .canonical import byte_digest, canonical_bytes, canonical_digest, require_canonical_document\\nfrom .crypto import b64url_decode, signature_payload, verify\\nfrom .errors import ConformanceError\\nfrom .linux_readiness import ARCHITECTURES, CASES, build_probe_request, require_time\\nfrom .live import ENVELOPE_DOMAIN, FIXED_RELEASE_TRUST, FIXED_TENANT_TRUST, _trust_key\\nfrom .live_backend_authority import COMMANDS, PACKET_ID, PACKET_DIGEST, binding_from_authority, verify_backend_authority\\nfrom .live_linux_boundary import (CHILD_UID, CHILD_GID, CgroupLease, LinuxSyscalls, credentials,\\n    ambient_custody, installed_process, process_identity, read_owned, read_owned_kit, require, validate_peer)\\nfrom .live_replay_store import ReplayStore, UnitReplayStore, open_directory\\nfrom .live_session import SCHEMA, validate_binding, validate_receipt, validate_session\\n\\n\\ndef utc_now():\\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\\\"microseconds\\\").replace(\\\"+00:00\\\", \\\"Z\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef _verify_reference_authority(envelope, release_raw, tenant_raw, now):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Verify BOTH signatures before opening any envelope-selected reference.\\n\\n    Capacity's independent third signature is verified after reading its exact\\n    now-authenticated reference. This early check grants no session/execution.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    instant = require_time(now, \\\"now\\\")\\n    require(envelope[\\\"packetId\\\"] == PACKET_ID and envelope[\\\"packetDigest\\\"] == PACKET_DIGEST\\n            and envelope[\\\"campaignId\\\"] == \\\"linux-baseline\\\"\\n            and envelope[\\\"commands\\\"] == [list(command) for command in COMMANDS]\\n            and instant < require_time(envelope[\\\"expiresAt\\\"], \\\"expiresAt\\\"), \\\"REFERENCE_AUTHORITY_SCOPE_INVALID\\\")\\n    require(byte_digest(release_raw) == envelope[\\\"releaseTrustStoreDigest\\\"]\\n            and byte_digest(tenant_raw) == envelope[\\\"tenantTrustStoreDigest\\\"], \\\"REFERENCE_TRUST_DIGEST_MISMATCH\\\")\\n    payload = signature_payload(ENVELOPE_DOMAIN, envelope, (\\\"platformSignature\\\", \\\"tenantSignature\\\"))\\n    selected = []\\n    for raw, role, field, signature, tenant, environment in (\\n            (release_raw, \\\"PLATFORM_RELEASE\\\", \\\"platformSignerKeyId\\\", \\\"platformSignature\\\", None, None),\\n            (tenant_raw, \\\"TENANT_LIVE_EXECUTION\\\", \\\"tenantSignerKeyId\\\", \\\"tenantSignature\\\",\\n             envelope[\\\"tenantId\\\"], envelope[\\\"environmentId\\\"])):\\n        trust = require_canonical_document(raw)\\n        key = _trust_key(trust, envelope[field], role, tenant, environment, instant)\\n        record = next(item for item in trust[\\\"keys\\\"] if item[\\\"keyId\\\"] == envelope[field])\\n        require(instant < require_time(record[\\\"validUntil\\\"], \\\"validUntil\\\"), \\\"REFERENCE_TRUST_EXPIRED\\\")\\n        require(verify(key, payload, b64url_decode(envelope[signature], expected_length=64)),\\n                \\\"REFERENCE_SIGNATURE_INVALID\\\")\\n        selected.append((key, record[\\\"owner\\\"]))\\n    require(selected[0][0] != selected[1][0] and selected[0][1] != selected[1][1], \\\"SIGNER_ROLES_NOT_INDEPENDENT\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass _Handle:\\n    __slots__ = (\\\"owner\\\", \\\"serial\\\")\\n\\n    def __init__(self, owner, serial):\\n        self.owner, self.serial = owner, serial\\n\\n    def __reduce__(self):\\n        raise TypeError(\\\"protected session handles are not serializable\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass _Lifecycle:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"State/ordering shared by native and explicit unit adapters, not authority.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    evidence_class = \\\"UNIT_VERIFICATION_ONLY\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, journal, boundary, clock=utc_now, monotonic=time.monotonic):\\n        self._journal, self._boundary = journal, boundary\\n        self._clock, self._monotonic = clock, monotonic\\n        self._active = None\\n        self._mutex = threading.Lock()\\n        self._serial = 0\\n        self._opened = False\\n\\n    def _now(self, active):\\n        now = self._clock()\\n        instant = require_time(now, \\\"now\\\")\\n        require(instant >= active[\\\"lastWall\\\"], \\\"SUPERVISOR_CLOCK_ROLLBACK\\\")\\n        active[\\\"lastWall\\\"] = instant\\n        require(instant < require_time(active[\\\"binding\\\"][\\\"notAfter\\\"], \\\"end\\\")\\n                and self._monotonic() < active[\\\"deadline\\\"], \\\"SUPERVISOR_EXPIRED\\\")\\n        return now\\n\\n    def _open(self, binding, plan):\\n        binding = validate_binding(binding)\\n        require(self._active is None and not self._opened, \\\"SUPERVISOR_BUSY\\\")\\n        now = self._clock()\\n        state = {**{k: v for k, v in binding.items() if k not in (\\\"notBefore\\\", \\\"notAfter\\\")},\\n                 \\\"schemaVersion\\\": SCHEMA, \\\"issuedAt\\\": binding[\\\"notBefore\\\"], \\\"expiresAt\\\": binding[\\\"notAfter\\\"],\\n \\\"SUPERVISOR_ARCHITECTURE_INVALID\\\")\\n        duration = (require_time(binding[\\\"notAfter\\\"], \\\"end\\\") - require_time(now, \\\"now\\\")).total_seconds()\\n        active = {\\\"binding\\\": binding, \\\"session\\\": state, \\\"plan\\\": plan, \\\"done\\\": {}, \\\"handle\\\": None,\\n                  \\\"deadline\\\": self._monotonic() + min(duration, 900), \\\"lastWall\\\": require_time(now, \\\"now\\\")}\\n        # No child, credential, or operation exists before durable reservation.\\n        self._journal.reserve_nonce(binding, now)\\n        self._opened = True\\n        self._active = active\\n        try:\\n            self._boundary.establish(binding, plan, active[\\\"deadline\\\"])\\n            self._boundary.check_peer()\\n            self._journal.running(binding, self._now(active))\\n            state[\\\"state\\\"] = \\\"RUNNING\\\"\\n            self._serial += 1\\n            handle = _Handle(self, self._serial)\\n \\\"SUPERVISOR_HANDLE_INVALID\\\")\\n        return active\\n\\n    def execute_fixed(self, handle, case_id, architecture):\\n        require(self._mutex.acquire(blocking=False), active[\\\"done\\\"],\\n                        \\\"SUPERVISOR_OPERATION_INVALID\\\")\\n                require(architecture == active[\\\"plan\\\"][\\\"target\\\"][\\\"architecture\\\"], \\\"SUPERVISOR_ARCHITECTURE_MISMATCH\\\")\\n                self._now(active)\\n                self._boundary.check_peer()\\n                request = self._boundary.request(case_id)\\n           self._boundary.check_peer()\\n                expected = active[\\\"plan\\\"][\\\"regressions\\\"] if case_id == \\\"FULL_PREDECESSOR_REGRESSION\\\" else {}\\n                receipt = validate_receipt(raw, active[\\\"session\\\"], active[\\\"binding\\\"], request, expected, now)\\n                active[\\\"done\\\"][case_id] = canonical_digest(receipt)\\n                if receipt[\\\"status\\\"] != \\\"PASS\\\":\\n                    self._terminate(\\\"FAILED\\\")\\n                elif len(active[\\\"done\\\"]) == len(CASES):\\n                    self._terminate(\\\"COMPLETED\\\")\\n                return {\\\"receipt\\\": receipt, \\\"evidenceClass\\\": self.evidence_class, \\\"nativeAcceptance\\\": False}\\n            except BaseException:\\n                if self._active is not None:\\n                    self._terminate(\\\"FAILED\\\")\\n                raise\\n        finally:\\n            self._mutex.release()\\n\\n    def _terminate(self, state):\\n        active = self._active\\n        if active is None:\\n            return\\n        # Invalidate first. Failure to clean up or persist never resurrects it.\\n        self._active = None\\n        now = self._clock()\\n        if (require_time(now, \\\"now\\\") >= require_time(active[\\\"binding\\\"][\\\"notAfter\\\"], \\\"end\\\")\\n                or self._monotonic() >= active[\\\"deadline\\\"]):\\n            # A monotonic timeout earlier than signed wall expiry is FAILED,\\n            # not a fabricated wall-time EXPIRED transition.\\n            state = \\\"EXPIRED\\\" if require_time(now, \\\"now\\\") >= require_time(active[\\\"binding\\\"][\\\"notAfter\\\"], \\\"end\\\") else \\\"FAILED\\\"\\n        try:\\n            self._boundary.cleanup()\\n        except BaseException:\\n            # Leave durable RESERVED/RUNNING consumed; never certify cleanup.\\n            raise\\n        self._journal.terminal(active[\\\"binding\\\"], state, now, canonical_digest(\\n            {\\\"state\\\": state, \\\"receipts\\\": active[\\\"done\\\"], \\\"nativeAcceptance\\\": False}))\\n\\n    def cancel(self, handle):\\n        # A concurrent execute is interrupted by the boundary's cancellation\\n        # primitive. No new operation can be admitted while its lock is held.\\n        self._get(handle)\\n        self._boundary.interrupt()\\n        with self._mutex:\\n            if self._active is not None:\\n                self._terminate(\\\"CANCELLED\\\")\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        with self._mutex:\\n            self._terminate(\\\"FAILED\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass UnitSupervisor(_Lifecycle):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Offline-only adapters; never accepted as a native installed context.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def open_session(self, binding, plan):\\n        require(type(self._journal) is UnitReplayStore, \\\"UNIT_STORAGE_REQUIRED\\\")\\n        with self._mutex:\\n            return self._open(binding, plan)\\n\\n\\nclass InstalledContext:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Factory-owned custody, not a dict or caller-provided FD/verified flag.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    __slots__ = (\\\"_owner\\\", \\\"_binding\\\", \\\"_plan\\\", \\\"_envelope\\\", \\\"_capacity\\\", \\\"_source_digest\\\", \\\"_root_fd\\\")\\n\\n    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):\\n        raise TypeError(\\\"only NativeSupervisor.open_session may construct installed context\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass _NativeChannel:\\n    def __init__(self, context, syscalls, lease):\\n        self.context, self.syscalls, self.lease = context, syscalls, lease\\n        self.parent_namespaces = {n: (Path(\\\"/proc/self/ns\\\") / n).stat().st_ino for n in (\\\"user\\\", \\\"mnt\\\", \\\"pid\\\", \\\"net\\\")}\\n        self.channel = self.child_pid = self.pidfd = self.peer = None\\n        self.cancelled = False\\n\\n    def establish(self, binding, plan, deadline):\\n        self.deadline = deadline\\n        parent, child = socket.socketpair(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_SEQPACKET)\\n        self.channel = parent\\n        gate_r = gate_w = None\\n        try:\\n            parent.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, 16, 1)  # SO_PASSCRED, per-message credentials\\n            gate_r, gate_w = os.pipe2(os.O_CLOEXEC)\\n            self.syscalls.prctl(36, 1)  # child subreaper, before any fork\\n            supervisor_pid = os.getpid()\\n            pid = os.fork()\\n        except BaseException:\\n            child.close()\\n            parent.close()\\n            for fd in (gate_r, gate_w):\\n                if fd is not None:\\n                    os.close(fd)\\n            raise\\n        if pid == 0:\\n            try:\\n                os.environ.clear()\\n                parent.close()\\n                os.close(gate_w)\\n                self.syscalls.protect_parent(supervisor_pid)\\n                require(os.read(gate_r, 1) == b\\\"G\\\", \\\"CHILD_GATE_CLOSED\\\")\\n                os.close(gate_r)\\n                os.setgroups([])\\n                self.syscalls.namespaces()\\n                # Parent is the only UID/GID-map writer; this is trusted setup,\\n                # before child checkout/import or credential access.\\n                child.send(b\\\"MAP\\\")\\n                require(child.recv(1) == b\\\"M\\\", \\\"CHILD_MAP_GATE_CLOSED\\\")\\n                os.setresgid(0, 0, 0)\\n                os.setresuid(0, 0, 0)\\n                self.syscalls.protect_parent(supervisor_pid)\\n                parent_identity_fd = os.pidfd_open(os.getpid(), 0)\\n                init = os.fork()\\n                if init:\\n                    os.close(parent_identity_fd)\\n                    child.close()\\n                    os.waitpid(init, 0)\\n                    os._exit(0)\\n                # PID-namespace init dies on intermediate parent death; kernel\\n                # kills namespace descendants even after a double fork.\\n                # getppid() is zero for a parent outside this PID namespace.\\n                # A retained pidfd closes the death-before-prctl race instead.\\n                self.syscalls.prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL)\\n                require(not select.select([parent_identity_fd], [], [], 0)[0], \\\"SUPERVISOR_PARENT_DIED\\\")\\n                os.close(parent_identity_fd)\\n                self.syscalls.readonly_root(self.context._root_fd)\\n                channel_fd = child.fileno()\\n                # Only this owned local channel survives. Never inherit a root,\\n                # cgroup, trust, journal, network or credential descriptor.\\n                os.closerange(0, channel_fd)\\n                os.closerange(channel_fd + 1, 1048576)\\n                self.syscalls.drop_privileges()\\n                self.syscalls.seccomp()\\n                child.send(b\\\"READY\\\")\\n                # The isolated custodian holds the channel until cancellation,\\n                # deadline or EOF. Probe execution is the later fixed proxy\\n                # component, not an arbitrary argv/callback in this packet.\\n                while time.monotonic() < deadline:\\n                    child.settimeout(max(0.001, deadline - time.monotonic()))\\n                    if child.recv(1) != b\\\"P\\\":\\n                        break\\n                    child.send(b\\\"P\\\")\\n                os._exit(0)\\n            except BaseException:\\n                os._exit(125)\\n        self.child_pid = pid\\n        child.close()\\n        os.close(gate_r)\\n        try:\\n            self.lease.attach(pid)\\n            os.write(gate_w, b\\\"G\\\")\\n        finally:\\n            os.close(gate_w)\\n        raw, cred = self._receive(8)\\n        require(raw == b\\\"MAP\\\" and cred[0] == pid and cred[1:] == (0, 0), \\\"CHILD_MAP_PEER_INVALID\\\")\\n        for name, value in ((\\\"setgroups\\\", b\\\"deny\\\"), (\\\"uid_map\\\", f\\\"0 {CHILD_UID} 1\\\".encode()),\\n                            (\\\"gid_map\\\", f\\\"0 {CHILD_GID} 1\\\".encode())):\\n            # Exact kernel child paths while the unreaped, owned child is gated.\\n            fd = os.open(f\\\"/proc/{pid}/{name}\\\", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)\\n            try:\\n                require(os.write(fd, value) == len(value), \\\"CHILD_MAP_WRITE_FAILED\\\")\\n            finally:\\n                os.close(fd)\\n        parent.send(b\\\"M\\\")\\n        raw, cred = self._receive(8)\\n        require(raw == b\\\"READY\\\" and cred[1:] == (CHILD_UID, CHILD_GID), \\\"CHILD_PEER_INVALID\\\")\\n        self.pidfd = os.pidfd_open(cred[0], 0)\\n        self.peer = process_identity(cred[0])\\n        require(self.peer[\\\"parent\\\"] == pid, \\\"CHILD_PARENT_MISMATCH\\\")\\n        self.channel_identity = os.fstat(parent.fileno()).st_ino\\n        self.check_peer()\\n\\n    def _receive(self, size):\\n        remaining = self.deadline - time.monotonic()\\n        require(remaining > 0 and not self.cancelled, \\\"SUPERVISOR_EXPIRED\\\")\\n        self.channel.settimeout(min(remaining, 1))\\n        while time.monotonic() < self.deadline and not self.cancelled:\\n            try:\\n                raw, ancillary, flags, _ = self.channel.recvmsg(size, socket.CMSG_SPACE(12) + socket.CMSG_SPACE(253 * 4))\\n                peer = credentials(ancillary, flags)\\n                require(raw, \\\"CHILD_CHANNEL_EOF\\\")\\n                return raw, peer\\n            except socket.timeout:\\n                continue\\n        raise ConformanceError(\\\"SUPERVISOR_EXPIRED\\\", \\\"bounded channel deadline reached\\\")\\n\\n    def check_peer(self):\\n        require(self.peer is not None and not self.cancelled\\n                and os.fstat(self.channel.fileno()).st_ino == self.channel_identity, \\\"PEER_CHANNEL_CHANGED\\\")\\n        require(not select.select([self.pidfd], [], [], 0)[0], \\\"PEER_DIED\\\")\\n        validate_peer(process_identity(self.peer[\\\"pid\\\"]), self.peer, self.parent_namespaces)\\n\\n    def request(self, case_id):\\n        return build_probe_request(self.context._envelope, self.context._capacity, self.context._plan, case_id)\\n\\n    def execute(self, request, deadline):\\n        self.check_peer()\\n        self.channel.send(b\\\"P\\\")\\n        raw, peer = self._receive(1)\\n        require(raw == b\\\"P\\\" and peer == (self.peer[\\\"pid\\\"], CHILD_UID, CHILD_GID), \\\"PEER_CHANNEL_CHANGED\\\")\\n        # Only the fixed, installed successor module is eligible. No registry,\\n        # module name, callback or command comes from caller data. It must enforce\\n        # the signed endpoint and server-side admission under CONF-LIVE-003.\\n        try:\\n            from .live_proxy_client import execute_protected\\n        except ImportError as exc:\\n            raise ConformanceError(\\\"PROTECTED_PROXY_UNAVAILABLE\\\", \\\"CONF-LIVE-003 is not installed\\\") from exc\\n        return execute_protected(request, self.context, deadline)\\n\\n    def interrupt(self):\\n        self.cancelled = True\\n        if self.lease.owned and not self.lease.closed:\\n            self.lease.write(\\\"cgroup.kill\\\", b\\\"1\\\")\\n\\n time.monotonic() + 10)\\n        finally:\\n            if self.channel is not None:\\n                self.channel.close()\\n                self.channel = None\\n            if self.pidfd is not None:\\n                os.close(self.pidfd)\\n                self.pidfd = None\\n\\n\\nclass NativeSupervisor(_Lifecycle):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fixed production dependencies; no unit/test/backend/path parameters.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    evidence_class = \\\"UNSIGNED_PROTECTED_RECEIPT_CANDIDATE\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        self._launcher_digest = installed_process()\\n        ambient_custody()\\n        self._syscalls = LinuxSyscalls()\\n        directory = open_directory(\\\"/var/lib/planeon/live-backend\\\", private=True)\\n        try:\\n            self._lease_fd = os.open(\\\"session.lock\\\", os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=directory)\\n            info = os.fstat(self._lease_fd)\\n            require(stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) and info.st_uid == info.st_gid == 0 and info.st_nlink == 1\\n                    and stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) == 0o600, \\\"SUPERVISOR_LEASE_INVALID\\\")\\n            fcntl.flock(self._lease_fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)\\n        except BaseException:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\\"_lease_fd\\\"):\\n                os.close(self._lease_fd)\\n            raise\\n        finally:\\n            os.close(directory)\\n        try:\\n            self._lease = CgroupLease()\\n            super().__init__(ReplayStore(), None)\\n        except BaseException:\\n            if hasattr(self, \\\"_lease\\\"):\\n                self._lease.close()\\n            os.close(self._lease_fd)\\n            raise\\n        self._context = None\\n        self._closed = False\\n\\n    def open_session(self, envelope_bytes, installed_context=None):\\n        # The sole context is generated here from independently owned files.\\n        # No caller-owned context (even another native instance's) is accepted.\\n        require(installed_context is None, \\\"CALLER_CONTEXT_FORBIDDEN\\\")\\n        with self._mutex:\\n            require(not self._closed and self._context is None, now=require_time(now, \\\"now\\\"))\\n            release_trust = read_owned(str(FIXED_RELEASE_TRUST))\\n            tenant_trust = read_owned(str(FIXED_TENANT_TRUST))\\n            _verify_reference_authority(envelope, release_trust, tenant_trust, now)\\n            capacity = read_owned(envelope[\\\"capacityAuthorizationFileReference\\\"], None, \\\"LINUX_ARCHITECTURE_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n            # The signed release already names each architecture's plan.\\n            plan_path = f\\\"campaigns/platform/linux-baseline/inputs/{architecture}.json\\\"\\n            match = [row for row in release[\\\"tree\\\"] if row[\\\"path\\\"] == plan_path]\\n            require(len(match) == 1, \\\"RELEASE_PLAN_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n            kit_sources = read_owned_kit(envelope[\\\"conformanceKitRoot\\\"], envelope[\\\"conformanceKitDigest\\\"])\\n            require(plan_path in kit_sources, \\\"RELEASE_PLAN_UNAVAILABLE\\\")\\n            plan_raw = kit_sources[plan_path]\\n            binding = binding_from_authority(envelope_bytes, capacity, release_trust, tenant_trust,\\n                release_bytes=release_raw, plan_bytes=plan_raw, architecture=architecture,\\n                expected_nonce=envelope[\\\"nonce\\\"], expected_tenant=envelope[\\\"tenantId\\\"],\\n                expected_environment=envelope[\\\"environmentId\\\"], expected_release=envelope[\\\"campaignReleaseDigest\\\"], now=now)\\n            for path_field, digest_field in ((\\\"packetFileReference\\\", \\\"packetDigest\\\"),\\n                    (\\\"campaignDefinitionFileReference\\\", \\\"campaignDefinitionDigest\\\"), (\\\"bundleFileReference\\\", \\\"bundleDigest\\\")):\\n                read_owned(envelope[path_field], expected_digest=envelope[digest_field])\\n            # All rootfs bytes must belong to the signed kit.
