authority\\n\\nAlpha 2 is **ONGOING**. This packet is source-only. It neither installs a\\nlauncher nor runs a native campaign. Unit receipts always retain\\n`nativeAcceptance=false`; a validated receipt is not tenant acceptance.\\nThis document describes the implementation and its acceptance obligations, not\\na claim that the obligations have passed. Exact commit/run evidence is recorded\\nseparately at PR closure.\\n\\n- Packet: CONF-LIVE-002, SHA-256 `05a6f03d19a1e12fafe278e5ec72c8adc6304766697b8682bc8d63092d47427f`.\\n- Meta authority: `3b1ea6b9b8514fbb51e37ba4703f5d06298bd938`.\\n- Immediate predecessor: CONF-LIVE-001, main `d734b80a3a5acebfd0e0d289437c0f53f3539054`,\\n  tree `5cf9cb3e1b286f6c16dcf77ccb4b5524ff568a44`, 120 files / 216 tests.\\n- Guide SHA-256: `1f1e62240ad6007c962e0a792787a6e5c569f6f72e3fde461868f72c77f034e0`.\\n- Trusted live-runner contract SHA-256:\\n  `69690c7bbe0899798f0c39e34e33c13dcd11fca470b8806be2c9c749a8622507`.\\n- Exactly seven additive paths. No predecessor, dispatcher, workflow, source\\n  lock, public schema, signature role or PORTING record changes.\\n\\n## Components and state\\n\\n| Component | Dependency and responsibility | State / failure |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| NativeSupervisor | Fixed installed launcher custody, three independent signature roles, exact signed files and kit, native adapter | One session per dedicated process; no caller context or backend argument |\\n| Native channel | Owned socketpair; per-message kernel credentials; pidfd, PID/start/cgroup/UID/GID/capability/namespace checks | Validate before and after blocking operations; death/EOF/cancellation invalidates handle |\\n| Cgroup lease | Preallocated empty root-owned cgroup-v2 leaf, finite CPU/memory/PID limits, exclusive session lock | Attach before releasing child gate; kill whole leaf, drain adopted descendants, prove populated=0 |\\n| Replay journal | Root-only no-follow file, exclusive flock, chained canonical JSONL, append + fsync + readback | RESERVED before child creation, RUNNING before operations, terminal only after cleanup |\\n| UnitSupervisor / UnitBoundary / UnitReplayStore | Explicit injected unit adapters | UNIT_VERIFICATION_ONLY, never installed authority |\\n| Fixed proxy successor | CONF-LIVE-003, later protected network/admission implementation | Missing module is PROTECTED_PROXY_UNAVAILABLE; no fallback |\\n\\nLifecycle:\\n\\n`VALIDATED_DATA -> durable RESERVED -> containment + peer checks -> RUNNING -> COMPLETED / FAILED / CANCELLED / EXPIRED`\\n\\nTerminal states have no outgoing edge. Only all ten fixed, distinct passing\\noperations can complete. A failed or unavailable operation closes the session;\\nan unavailable operation's receipt stays NOT_RUN_ENV_UNAVAILABLE. Cancellation\\nracing an already executing operation may end in FAILED (interrupted I/O) or\\nCANCELLED; neither permits reuse. Cleanup failure or clock rollback can leave\\nthe durable record RESERVED/RUNNING **consumed**, with no fabricated terminal\\nreceipt. Journal read errors, torn writes, uncertain fsync/readback, full storage\\nand custody changes all refuse execution. No truncation, repair, nonce expiry,\\nrotation or automatic lease recovery is implemented.\\n\\nAn append failure before any byte reaches storage may leave an empty journal\\nafter process restart. That is safe only because execution never precedes a\\nsuccessful durable reservation. Any complete or partial reservation survives\\nas consumed or as a globally refusing corrupted journal. Root/operator\\ntampering, disk rollback, hardware lying about fsync and hostile kernels are\\noutside this source candidate's trust boundary; production backup/restore must\\npreserve replay history and never restore a reusable old nonce ledger.\\n\\n## Native boundary and custody\\n\\nThe production constructor accepts no path, executable, callback, module name,\\nstorage adapter, FD, token or verified flag. It requires Linux, root UID/GID,\\none thread, no preexisting children and default SIGCHLD disposition; the\\nmodule must be loaded from the exact installed launcher archive and its\\nindependently signed root manifest must verify. The unchanged legacy launcher\\ncontinues to refuse live execution until the separately owned CONF-LIVE-006\\nbridge and external installation gates are complete.\\n\\nThe native entry also rejects ambient credentials, proxy/import variables and\\nopen descriptors beyond nonsocket stdio. Only PATH, HOME, USER, LOGNAME, LANG,\\nLC_ALL, TZ and the fixed HARNESS_LIVE_EXECUTION_ENVELOPE input may be present;\\nnone is an authorization flag. The child clears that remaining environment.\\nManifest/public-key/signature/launcher bytes are read once under no-follow\\ncustody, verified with the unchanged signature/key contract, and retained by\\nthe dedicated process. Repeated internal native constructors use that verified\\nsnapshot; the envelope's launcher digest must equal it.\\n\\nBoth platform and tenant signatures and fixed trust-store digests are verified\\nbefore opening any envelope-selected reference, including the capacity file.\\nOnly then may that exact digest-bound capacity reference be read and its\\nindependent CAPACITY_OPERATOR signature and complete binding verified. Invalid\\nor forged references therefore never reach a file open; none grants execution.\\n\\nAuthority references use retained no-follow directory/file descriptors, exact\\nroot ownership, no write bits, single-link regular files, bounded reads and\\ninode/metadata rechecks. The signed kit is enumerated independently, with each\\nfile read once. It rejects symlinks, hard links, device crossings, unsupported\\nobjects, writable files, path aliases and inventory/digest drift. No warm\\ncheckout or ambient root filesystem is used as a kit. The source candidate's\\nFixed bounds are 4 MiB/file, 64 MiB total file data, 4096 files, 8192 total\\nentries, 4096 entries per directory and depth 32; the release must fit these\\nbounds or be unavailable.\\nA root-owned, nonwritable `rootfs/` inside that exact signed kit is required;\\nthere is no host filesystem fallback. Packaging/prerequisite qualification is\\nnot supplied by this packet.\\n\\nBefore any child checkout or credential access, trusted setup:\\n\\n1. Creates a supervisor-owned SOCK_SEQPACKET socketpair and a closed child gate;\\n   marks the dedicated parent a child subreaper.\\n2. Forks a trusted setup child and attaches it to the exclusively leased cgroup\\n   before releasing the gate. An ambiguous attach still requires cleanup.\\n3. Clears supplementary groups and creates user, mount, PID and network\\n   namespaces; the parent writes exact single-ID UID/GID maps to host 65532.\\n4. Re-arms parent-death SIGKILL after credential changes. PID-namespace init\\n   retains a parent pidfd to close the death-before-prctl race even when\\n   getppid() is zero across the namespace boundary.\\n5. Makes mount propagation private; recursively bind-mounts the signed rootfs\\n   read-only/nosuid/nodev and chroots into it. Closes all descriptors except the\\n   owned local channel; removes capabilities and enables no_new_privs.\\n6. Installs architecture-checked classic-BPF seccomp rules. Direct socket\\n   creation/connect/bind/listen, namespace escape, ptrace, BPF, userfaultfd,\\n   io_uring and pidfd_getfd paths are denied on amd64/arm64. x32 is rejected.\\n   clone3 returns ENOSYS; ordinary clone/fork is allowed only inside the\\n   PID namespace, limits and cgroup. This is a layered boundary, not a claim\\n   that a syscall denylist alone is a complete sandbox.\\n7. Reports READY through per-message SCM_CREDENTIALS. The parent checks the\\n   child's kernel identity, exact cgroup, distinct namespaces, mapped UID/GID,\\n   zero capability sets, seccomp and no_new_privs, and retains a pidfd.\\n\\nSO_PEERCRED from socketpair creation is deliberately not used as post-fork\\nsender identity. Truncated, duplicate or unexpected ancillary messages fail;\\nall received SCM_RIGHTS descriptors are closed and never become authority.\\nOnly the supervisor-created channel is used, with checks around every bounded\\noperation. The isolated custodian presently accepts only a fixed ping byte;\\nit does **not** yet execute probes or load tenant credentials.\\n\\nThe child uses an absolute monotonic deadline (at most 900 seconds and never\\npast signed expiry), recalculated before each receive so pings cannot extend\\nits life. Parent death kills the trusted intermediate parent and namespace\\ninit; PID namespace teardown kills remaining namespace processes. An alive\\nsupervisor's expiry/cancel/close path writes cgroup.kill and drains direct and\\nadopted children until both wait custody and cgroup emptiness are proved.\\nKernel/init reaping after supervisor death is a **native qualification gate**,\\nnot something offline mocks establish. After a parent crash, an occupied\\npreallocated leaf blocks the next session instead of being silently cleared.\\n\\n## Operator prerequisites — not performed here\\n\\nThe separately authorized native operator must provide a supported native\\n64-bit Linux host, Python 3.12.14 and the exact signed launcher, cgroup v2\\nincluding cgroup.kill, mount_setattr recursive read-only support, user/mount/\\nPID/network namespaces, pidfd, prctl/capset/seccomp, and enabled finite CPU/\\nmemory/PID controllers. Unsupported primitives have no subprocess/emulation/\\nuncontained fallback. Missing prerequisites are NOT_RUN_ENV_UNAVAILABLE, never\\nPASS; the later bridge owns mapping internal prerequisite exceptions into the\\nunchanged campaign result format.\\n\\nFixed, precreated paths (not user request parameters):\\n\\n- `/sys/fs/cgroup/planeon-live/session`: empty non-delegated cgroup-v2 domain.\\n- `/var/lib/planeon/live-backend`: root:root 0700.\\n- `session.lock` and `replay.jsonl` under that directory: root:root 0600,\\n  precreated regular files, no hard links. The journal starts empty once;\\n  never recreate it to clear consumed nonces.\\n- Existing root manifest, release trust and tenant trust fixed mounts under\\n  the unchanged trusted live-runner contract.\\n\\nNo install command, sudo policy, cluster/VM provisioning, rootfs creation,\\ncredential import, cloud API, hosted runner or network policy mutation is part\\nof this source run.\\n\\n## Handoff to CONF-LIVE-003 and remaining roadmap\\n\\nThe only production transport hook is the fixed relative import\\n`live_proxy_client.execute_protected(request, installed_context, deadline)`.\\nIt is intentionally absent until its owning packet. Caller data never chooses\\na transport/module/URL/argv. The context is constructed only by NativeSupervisor\\nand retains its owning supervisor, validated binding, plan, envelope, capacity,\\nsource digest and owned rootfs FD. These are private in-process custody data,\\nnot serializable session authority.\\n\\nThe privileged broker is distinct from the isolated child. CONF-LIVE-003 must\\nindependently enforce the single signed endpoint, TLS identity, no discovery/\\nredirect/proxy environment, bounded request/response/deadline, short-lived\\ncredential custody and server-side zero-cost quota admission. A live peer or\\nsuccessful ping grants none of those permissions by itself. CONF-LIVE-004\\nowns the ten real fixed probes. CONF-LIVE-005 owns deterministic candidate\\npackaging; CONF-LIVE-006 owns the closed integration delta. External operator\\ninstallation, native amd64/arm64 Linux, OCP/Kubernetes runtime, assurance and\\ntenant acceptance remain separate WAITING gates.\\n\\n## Offline verification and evidence boundaries\\n\\nRun only the packet's exact eight direct argv through the signed, root-owned\\noffline launcher in one deny-all-outbound process tree: all six unittest roots,\\nthen campaign linux-baseline and evidence-verify linux-baseline. No separate\\ntest execution or native syscall execution is permitted during coding/CI.\\n\\nNew tests cover reserve-before-child ordering; nonce contention; crashes before,\\nduring and after append/fsync/readback; consumed restart history; replay chain,\\nscope and timing; peer identity/FD spoofing; per-message credential rejection;\\nBPF instruction behavior for both ABIs and IPv4/IPv6/DNS/metadata denial;\\nnamespace-clone escape filtering; adapter failure ordering; cgroup attach\\nambiguity, adopted-child reaping and incomplete cleanup; one-operation locks,\\ncancellation, expired/late I/O and rollback; factory injection and unsigned\\nevidence. Syscalls are explicitly mocked; no test result is native\\ncontainment evidence. Inventory tests retain original 103/120, corrected\\n106/150, successor 110/170 and immediate 120/216 file/test checkpoints, including\\nfresh isolated discovery of all six roots without omission/skip/xfail.\\n\\nKeep local-source checks, GitHub CI, PR merge, local exact-main replay,\\ncandidate artifact/SBOM, installed preflight, native runtime, assurance and\\ntenant acceptance as independent states. Roll back this unconsumed increment\\nby a reviewed revert; after successor consumption use a corrective packet.\\nDo not alter installations, tenant resources, trust or replay history.\\n\\n## Design references\\n\\n- [Linux cgroup-v2 documentation](https://docs.kernel.org/admin-guide/cgroup-v2.html):\\n  process inheritance, resource limits and whole-cgroup kill behavior.\\n- [Linux seccomp filter documentation](https://www.kernel.org/doc/html/latest/userspace-api/seccomp_filter.html):\\n  architecture checks, no_new_privs and layered confinement.\\n- [Unix-domain socket credentials](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/unix.7.html):\\n  per-message SCM_CREDENTIALS versus socket-creation credentials.\\n- [User namespaces](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/user_namespaces.7.html):\\n  UID/GID mapping and supplementary-group ordering.\\n- [Parent-death signal](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/PR_SET_PDEATHSIG.2const.html)\\n  and [PID namespaces](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man7/pid_namespaces.7.html):\\n  re-arming, death races and PID-namespace init teardown.\\n- [Recursive mount attributes](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/mount_setattr.2.html):\\n  recursive read-only/nosuid/nodev enforcement.\\n\\n## Immutable immediate predecessor inventory\\n\\nThis is inert provenance data, never executable configuration. Its raw UTF-8\\nJSON (without a trailing newline) has SHA-256\\n`f2bc15e777afa6545c3fcfcdd63a0ddcba3d162783767083acb59e1744b115ac`, pinned independently in the new supervisor test.\\nOnly the previously accepted exact CONF-LIVE-006 hook proof can account for\\nthat final launcher delta; an integration filename alone never grants it.\\n\\n<!-- CONF-LIVE-001-BASELINE -->\\n```json\\n{\\\"evidenceClass\\\":\\\"SOURCE_INVENTORY_ONLY\\\",\\\"nativeAcceptance\\\":false,\\\"commit\\\":\\\"d734b80a3a5acebfd0e0d289437c0f53f3539054\\\",\\\"tree\\\":\\\"5cf9cb3e1b286f6c16dcf77ccb4b5524ff568a44\\\",\\\"files\\\":{\\\".github/workflows/verify.yml\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"48af47e6d1ca5c5198d934c1dd10c3ba947c3192\\\",\\\"size\\\":615,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:91090dc69c12837e8b73eb41a868b3afca7dcb34b304c7b409ac716310ddd3a6\\\"},\\\".gitignore\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"d9d863a13c2bfc70183b59f43ddfa236f7fa7e34\\\",\\\"size\\\":158,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:0672c3d34147eb3a4b4aa4298d4d88f647d6d28aa22cffdb705508df11bda33a\\\"},\\\"AGENTS.md\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"3bef97c6f3d501a0b5528179880053f787147de2\\\",\\\"size\\\":3064,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:0b8faaf320feae214a47000b924c9a9c717e73e6d220edf1d16f0ff14381e843\\\"},\\\"CONTRIBUTING.md\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"21f949ba69f148982b99313a6fc319ed5caf3527\\\",\\\"size\\\":460,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:d947eeaf23f47ad60e26bb2e0f236f08a8bd74ca7f8e80d378248ef4477d5964\\\"},\\\"LICENSE\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"94f474d4d34ef439ac1bb0f1961d5cc9e9096c7e\\\",\\\"size\\\":774,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:2d3b806e6fd270f11819d0f797f721747adb0d497760e1b9053b6cd1fae4cf54\\\"},\\\"Makefile\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"729893b50ddf19f0be9a3024f435af789a283897\\\",\\\"size\\\":661,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:bb652db371113bab5d9d8924f0b10b1f85793c0cc84178d447ab1095f4e7b981\\\"},\\\"NOTICE\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"0c12f4d5afbb8d0b984d4e3b094735be11607cf5\\\",\\\"size\\\":169,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:a70fc36aa7b6f295c4a662b6443a0599c4c89e9b6e62feb29763967d79ce382f\\\"},\\\"PORTING.yaml\\\":{\\\"mode\\\":\\\"100644\\\",\\\"blob\\\":\\\"cdd098dabfe545c4f513a6821d32cb6da17ee047\\\",\\\"size\\\":253,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"sha256:69af26b731e28920bb4cc5dd25f6d  }\n}\n","evidenceClass":"SOURCE_DELTA_ONLY","packetId":"CONF-FIX-004","packetSha256":"6a41b419c4e5b02311e148b846d339216f1b512cf8025ae75de77e72ea3d8db0","schemaVersion":"planeon.internal.custody-source-delta/v1","sources":{"src/harness_conformance/live_linux_boundary.py":{"afterSha256":"fadec5982727ab5af7806753ce6814d7b93a13b9c5a7d426334a88cada03a1d4","append":"\n\ndef _capture():\n    \"\"\"Process-private registry, never a caller-supplied descriptor allowlist.\"\"\"\n    custody = getattr(_capture, \"current\", None)\n    if custody is not None:\n        require(type(custody) is _RetainedCustody, \"CUSTODY_OWNER_INVALID\")\n        custody.owner_check()\n    return custody\n\n\ndef _begin_custody(owner):\n    from .live_supervisor import NativeSupervisor\n    require(type(owner) is NativeSupervisor\n            and sys._getframe(1).f_code is NativeSupervisor.__init__.__code__\n            and sys._getframe(1).f_locals.get(\"self\") is owner,\n            \"CUSTODY_FACTORY_REQUIRED\")\n    require(getattr(_capture, \"current\", None) is None, \"CUSTODY_ALREADY_OWNED\")\n    custody = _RetainedCustody(owner)\n    _capture.current = custody\n    return custody\n\n\ndef _owned_custody(owner):\n    custody = _capture()\n    require(custody is not None and custody.owner is owner, \"CUSTODY_OWNER_INVALID\")\n    return custody\n\n\ndef _custody_identity(info):\n    return (info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_uid, info.st_gid, info.st_mode,\n            info.st_nlink, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns)\n\n\nclass _RetainedCustody:\n    \"\"\"Single close owner for bounded, read-once authority and full ancestry.\n\n    Instances alone grant nothing: the factory registry, current process, fixed\n    installed verification and active supervisor must all agree. No raw-FD API.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, owner):\n        import threading\n        self.owner, self.pid, self.thread = owner, os.getpid(), threading.get_ident()\n        self.handles, self.files = {}, {}\n        self.total = 0\n        self.manifest_digest = None\n        self.kit_root = self.kit_digest = None\n        self.kit_sources = {}\n        self.closed = self.sealed = False\n\n    def __reduce__(self):\n        raise TypeError(\"retained custody is not serializable\")\n\n    def owner_check(self):\n        import threading\n        from .live_supervisor import NativeSupervisor\n        require(not self.closed and type(self.owner) is NativeSupervisor\n                and self.pid == os.getpid() and self.thread == threading.get_ident()\n                and getattr(_capture, \"current\", None) is self, \"CUSTODY_OWNER_INVALID\")\n\n    def path(self, path):\n        require(type(path) is str and path.startswith(\"/\") and \"\\\\\" not in path\n                and len(path.encode(\"utf-8\")) <= 4096\n                and all(p not in (\"\", \".\", \"..\") for p in path[1:].split(\"/\"))\n                and len(path[1:].split(\"/\")) <= 64, \"CUSTODY_PATH_INVALID\")\n        return path[1:].split(\"/\")\n\n    def acquire(self, path, parent, name, directory, strict=False):\n        self.owner_check()\n        require(not self.sealed and path not in self.handles and len(self.handles) < 8448,\n                \"CUSTODY_DESCRIPTOR_LIMIT\")\n        flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NONBLOCK\n        if directory:\n            flags |= os.O_DIRECTORY\n        fd = os.open(name, flags, dir_fd=parent)\n        # Register immediately: even a failing fstat owns a close attempt.\n        row = dict(fd=fd, parent=parent, name=name, identity=None)\n        self.handles[path] = row\n        require(2 < fd < 1048576, \"CUSTODY_DESCRIPTOR_RANGE\")\n        info = os.fstat(fd)\n        row[\"identity\"] = _custody_identity(info)\n        require((stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) if directory else stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode))\n                and info.st_uid == info.st_gid == 0\n                and (directory or info.st_nlink == 1)\n                and not stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) & (0o222 if strict or not directory else 0o022),\n                \"CUSTODY_FILE_INVALID\")\n        self.check_row(row)\n        return fd\n\n    def directory(self, path, strict=False):\n        self.owner_check()\n        parts = self.path(path)\n        if \"/\" not in self.handles:\n            self.acquire(\"/\", None, \"/\", True)\n        parent, current = self.handles[\"/\"][\"fd\"], \"\"\n        for part in parts:\n            current += \"/\" + part\n            if current not in self.handles:\n                self.acquire(current, parent, part, True, strict and current == path)\n            row = self.handles[current]\n            self.check_row(row)\n            require(stat.S_ISDIR(row[\"identity\"][4]), \"CUSTODY_DIRECTORY_REQUIRED\")\n            parent = row[\"fd\"]\n        if strict:\n            require(not stat.S_IMODE(self.handles[path][\"identity\"][4]) & 0o222, \"KIT_CUSTODY_INVALID\")\n        return parent\n\n    def check_row(self, row):\n        expected = row[\"identity\"]\n        require(expected is not None\n                and _custody_identity(os.fstat(row[\"fd\"])) == expected\n                and _custody_identity(os.stat(row[\"name\"], dir_fd=row[\"parent\"], follow_symlinks=False)) == expected,\n                \"CUSTODY_FILE_CHANGED\")\n\n    def check(self):\n        self.owner_check()\n        for row in self.handles.values():\n            self.check_row(row)\n\n    def checked_fds(self):\n        self.check()\n        return {row[\"fd\"] for row in self.handles.values()}\n\n    def read(self, path, maximum, expected_digest, expected_mode):\n        try:\n            self.check()\n            self.path(path)\n            require(type(maximum) is int and 0 < maximum <= 4194304, \"CUSTODY_READ_LIMIT\")\n            if path not in self.files:\n                require(not self.sealed and len(self.files) < 4112, \"CUSTODY_FILE_LIMIT\")\n                parent_path = path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[0]\n                if not parent_path:\n                    if \"/\" not in self.handles:\n                        self.acquire(\"/\", None, \"/\", True)\n                    parent = self.handles[\"/\"][\"fd\"]\n                else:\n                    parent = self.directory(parent_path)\n                fd = self.acquire(path, parent, path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[1], False)\n                size = self.handles[path][\"identity\"][6]\n                require(0 <= size <= maximum and self.total + size <= 100663296, \"CUSTODY_READ_LIMIT\")\n                data = bytearray()\n                while len(data) <= size:\n                    chunk = os.read(fd, min(65536, size + 1 - len(data)))\n                    self.check()\n                    if not chunk:\n                        break\n                    data.extend(chunk)\n                require(len(data) == size, \"CUSTODY_FILE_CHANGED\")\n                self.files[path] = bytes(data)\n                self.total += size\n            raw = self.files[path]\n            require(len(raw) <= maximum, \"CUSTODY_READ_LIMIT\")\n            require(expected_mode is None or stat.S_IMODE(self.handles[path][\"identity\"][4]) == expected_mode,\n                    \"CUSTODY_MODE_INVALID\")\n            require(expected_digest is None or byte_digest(raw) == expected_digest, \"CUSTODY_DIGEST_MISMATCH\")\n            return raw\n        except BaseException:\n            self.close()\n            raise\n\n    def read_kit(self, root, expected_digest):\n        try:\n            self.check()\n            require(not self.sealed and self.kit_root is None, \"KIT_ALREADY_READ\")\n            root_fd = self.directory(root, strict=True)\n            device = self.handles[root][\"identity\"][0]\n            rows, sources = [], {}\n            entries = total = 0\n            def walk(path, prefix, depth):\n                nonlocal entries, total\n                require(depth <= 32, \"KIT_DEPTH_EXCEEDED\")\n                fd = self.directory(path, strict=True)\n                require(self.handles[path][\"identity\"][0] == device, \"KIT_MOUNT_OR_CUSTODY_INVALID\")\n                names = sorted(os.listdir(fd), key=lambda name: name.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n                self.check()\n                require(len(names) <= 4096, \"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\")\n                entries += len(names)\n                require(entries <= 8192, \"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\")\n                for name in names:\n                    require(type(name) is str and name not in (\"\", \".\", \"..\")\n                            and \"/\" not in name and \"\\\\\" not in name, \"KIT_PATH_INVALID\")\n                    info = os.stat(name, dir_fd=fd, follow_symlinks=False)\n                    require(info.st_dev == device and info.st_uid == info.st_gid == 0\n                            and not stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) & 0o222, \"KIT_CUSTODY_INVALID\")\n                    child, relative = path + \"/\" + name, prefix + name\n                    if stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode):\n                        walk(child, relative + \"/\", depth + 1)\n                    else:\n                        require(stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) and info.st_nlink == 1\n                                and 0 <= info.st_size <= 4194304, \"KIT_FILE_INVALID\")\n                        require(len(rows) < 4096 and total + info.st_size <= 67108864, \"KIT_INVENTORY_FULL\")\n                        raw = self.read(child, 4194304, None, None)\n                        require(self.handles[child][\"identity\"] == _custody_identity(info), \"KIT_CHANGED\")\n                        sources[relative] = raw\n                        total += len(raw)\n                        rows.append(dict(mode=f\"{stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode):04o}\", path=relative,\n                                         sha256=byte_digest(raw), size=len(raw)))\n                self.check()\n            walk(root, \"\", 0)\n            rows.sort(key=lambda row: row[\"path\"].encode(\"utf-8\"))\n            require(canonical_digest(rows, \"planeon.harness-live-tree/v1alpha1\") == expected_digest,\n                    \"KIT_DIGEST_MISMATCH\")\n            require(root + \"/rootfs\" in self.handles\n                    and stat.S_ISDIR(self.handles[root + \"/rootfs\"][\"identity\"][4]), \"KIT_ROOTFS_REQUIRED\")\n            self.kit_root, self.kit_digest, self.kit_sources = root, expected_digest, sources\n            return dict(sources)\n        except BaseException:\n            self.close()\n            raise\n\n    def require_bytes(self, expected):\n        self.check()\n        require(all(type(raw) is bytes and self.files.get(path) == raw for path, raw in expected.items()),\n                \"CUSTODY_INCOMPLETE\")\n\n    def seal(self, expected, kit_root, kit_digest, kit_sources):\n        self.require_bytes(expected)\n        require(self.manifest_digest is not None and not self.sealed\n                and self.kit_root == kit_root and self.kit_digest == kit_digest\n                and self.kit_sources == kit_sources and bool(self.kit_sources), \"CUSTODY_INCOMPLETE\")\n        from types import MappingProxyType\n        self.files = MappingProxyType(dict(self.files))\n        self.kit_sources = MappingProxyType(dict(self.kit_sources))\n        self.sealed = True\n        return self.handles[kit_root + \"/rootfs\"][\"fd\"]\n\n    def close(self):\n        if self.closed:\n            return\n        self.closed = True\n        # Invalidate before any close. Never retry a descriptor number that the\n        # OS may have released even when close reports an error.\n        if getattr(_capture, \"current\", None) is self:\n            _capture.current = None\n        rows, self.handles = self.handles, {}\n        self.files, self.kit_sources = {}, {}\n        failure = None\n        for row in reversed(tuple(rows.values())):\n            try:\n                expected = row[\"identity\"]\n                if expected is not None:\n                    info = os.fstat(row[\"fd\"])\n                    require((info.st_dev, info.st_ino, stat.S_IFMT(info.st_mode))\n                            == (expected[0], expected[1], stat.S_IFMT(expected[4])), \"CUSTODY_DESCRIPTOR_REUSED\")\n                os.close(row[\"fd\"])\n            except BaseException as exc:\n                if failure is None:\n                    failure = exc\n        if failure is not None:\n            raise failure\n","beforeSha256":"40f7a874204c550f0ac9f75644d2657c74335e16d518b966628a279148aa4324","regions":{"_installed_manifest_digest":"def _installed_manifest_digest():\n    \"\"\"Retain independently verified installed bytes; Failure to clean up or persist never resurrects it.\n        self._active = None\n        failure = None\n        try:\n            import math\n            now = self._clock()\n            instant, monotonic = require_time(now, \"now\"), self._monotonic()\n            require(instant >= active[\"lastWall\"], \"SUPERVISOR_CLOCK_ROLLBACK\")\n            require(type(monotonic) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(monotonic)\n                    and monotonic >= active[\"lastMonotonic\"], \"SUPERVISOR_MONOTONIC_ROLLBACK\")\n            if instant >= require_time(active[\"binding\"][\"notAfter\"], \"end\"):\n                state = \"EXPIRED\"\n            elif monotonic >= active[\"deadline\"]:\n                state = \"FAILED\"\n        except BaseException as exc:\n            failure = exc\n        try:\n            self._boundary.cleanup()\n        except BaseException as exc:\n            if failure is None:\n                failure = exc\n        if failure is not None:\n            # Consumed nonce, no terminal claim when time or cleanup is unproven.\n            raise failure\n        self._journal.terminal(active[\"binding\"], state, now, canonical_digest(\n            {\"state\": state, \"receipts\": active[\"done\"], \"nativeAcceptance\": False}))\n","_NativeChannel.check_peer":"    def check_peer(self):\n        require(type(self) is _NativeChannel and type(self.context) is InstalledContext\n                and type(self.context._owner) is NativeSupervisor, \"CUSTODY_CONTEXT_INVALID\")\n        self.context._owner._now(self.context._owner._active)\n        require(self.peer is not None and not self.cancelled\n                and os.fstat(self.channel.fileno()).st_ino == self.channel_identity, \"PEER_CHANNEL_CHANGED\")\n        require(not select.select([self.pidfd], [], [], 0)[0], \"PEER_DIED\")\n        validate_peer(process_identity(self.peer[\"pid\"]), self.peer, self.parent_namespaces)\n","_NativeChannel.cleanup":"    def cleanup(self):\n        self.cancelled = True\n        failure = None\n        try:\n            if self.child_pid is not None and self.lease.owned and not self.lease.closed:\n                self.lease.kill_and_reap(self.child_pid, time.monotonic() + 10)\n        except BaseException as exc:\n            failure = exc\n        channel, pidfd = self.channel, self.pidfd\n        self.channel = self.pidfd = None\n        operations = []\n        if channel is not None:\n            operations.append(channel.close)\n        if pidfd is not None:\n            operations.append(lambda: os.close(pidfd))\n        # Partial construction may have no context; absence permits cleanup\n        # only, never any check_peer/request/execute authority.\n        context = getattr(self, \"context\", None)\n        if type(context) is InstalledContext:\n            context._root_fd = None\n            operations.append(context._custody.close)\n        for operation in operations:\n            try:\n                operation()\n            except BaseException as exc:\n                if failure is None:\n                    failure = exc\n        if failure is not None:\n            raise failure\n","_NativeChannel.execute":"    def execute(self, request, deadline):\n        self.check_peer()\n        require(type(request) is dict and request.get(\"operation\") in CASES\n                and request == self.request(request[\"operation\"]), \"SUPERVISOR_OPERATION_INVALID\")\n        self.channel.send(b\"P\")\n        raw, peer = self._receive(1)\n        require(raw == b\"P\" and peer == (self.peer[\"pid\"], CHILD_UID, CHILD_GID), \"PEER_CHANNEL_CHANGED\")\n        self.check_peer()\n        require(deadline == self.context._owner._active[\"deadline\"], \"SUPERVISOR_DEADLINE_CHANGED\")\n        # Only the fixed, installed successor module is eligible. No registry,\n        # module name, callback or command comes from caller data. It must enforce\n        # the signed endpoint and server-side admission under CONF-LIVE-003.\n        try:\n            from .live_proxy_client import execute_protected\n        except ImportError as exc:\n            raise ConformanceError(\"PROTECTED_PROXY_UNAVAILABLE\", \"CONF-LIVE-003 is not installed\") from exc\n        result = execute_protected(request, self.context, deadline)\n        self.check_peer()\n        return result\n","_NativeChannel.request":"    def request(self, case_id):\n        self.check_peer()\n        return build_probe_request(self.context._envelope, self.context._capacity, self.context._plan, case_id)\n"}}},"tests":{"tests/live_backend/test_linux_boundary.py":{"afterSha256":"3ac00b5cc93c4d3ad2a3503c07cecbca96817cb29e37726364fffc7f30ab3f34","beforeSha256":"46b24fd49840a4779da89d6d0e7f85a862107bafaae309b9f371ae37a88dda9b","newTestIds":["RetainedBoundaryTests.test_ambient_unknown_descriptor_and_forked_registry_never_authorize","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_close_error_attempts_all_handles_once_and_invalidates_first","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_closed_recycled_descriptor_is_never_closed_as_new_authority","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_each_retained_file_and_ancestor_substitution_is_detected","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_every_custody_metadata_field_is_rechecked","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_fixed_identity_read_once_and_cached_digest_cannot_replace_registry","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_kit_rootfs_is_borrowed_once_and_signed_bytes_are_immutable","RetainedBoundaryTests.test_native_constructor_exhaustion_or_bad_installed_read_closes_every_handle"]},"tests/live_backend/test_supervisor.py":{"afterSha256":"4eaf42ce64255fa192ae5ec72e3a0767388723c8149fd1159588993899785ebd","beforeSha256":"48e867a3c53f3c7262e1b4e7adae621532a25b8c82fe7ab794f6b13eadc70864","newTestIds":["CustodySourceProofTests.test_actual_127_path_inventory_preserves_other_122_complete_file_bytes","CustodySourceProofTests.test_exact_five_path_delta_uses_pinned_data_only_oracle","CustodySourceProofTests.test_oracle_rejects_snapshot_prefix_scope_and_source_substitution","CustodySourceProofTests.test_original_279_methods_and_every_added_method_are_freshly_collected","RetainedSupervisorTests.test_cancel_expiry_failure_and_close_never_reuse_nonce_or_descriptors","RetainedSupervisorTests.test_cleanup_failure_still_releases_remaining_resources_without_terminal_success","RetainedSupervisorTests.test_data_only_mocked_readers_and_cached_digest_never_form_native_context","RetainedSupervisorTests.test_each_authority_kit_member_and_full_ancestor_change_refuses_before_hook","RetainedSupervisorTests.test_each_signer_expiry_
